 3376  from pathlib import Path
 3377  import os
 3378  base = Path('outpaint_images/test_intermediates')
 3379  out_seq = base / '_ordered_3x25_frames'
 3380  out_seq.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 3381  # clean previous frame links/files
 3382  for p in out_seq.glob('frame_*.png'):
 3383      p.unlink()
 3384  suffixes = ['prior_x0_hat', 'data_x0_proj', 'renoise_xt']
 3385  missing = []
 3386  idx = 1
 3387  for step in range(1, 26):
 3388      for suf in suffixes:
 3389          src = base / f'step_{step:03d}_{suf}.png'
 3390          if not src.exists():
 3391              missing.append(str(src))
 3392              continue
 3393          dst = out_seq / f'frame_{idx:05d}.png'
 3394          os.symlink(os.path.relpath(src, out_seq), dst)
 3395          idx += 1
 3396  print(f'Expected frames: {25*3}')
 3397  print(f'Created frames : {idx-1}')
 3398  if missing:
 3399      print('Missing files:')
 3400      for m in missing:
 3401          print(m)
 3402      raise SystemExit(2)
 3403  print('Frame sequence ready:', out_seq)
 3404  PY

 3408  LIST="$DIR/ordered_triplet_001_100.txt"
 3409  OUT="$DIR/steps_triplet_001_100.mp4"
 3410  : > "$LIST"
 3411  for y in $(seq 1 100); do   y3=$(printf "%03d" "$y");   prior="$DIR/step_${y3}_prior_x0_hat.png";   data="$DIR/step_${y3}_data_x0_proj.png";   renoise="$DIR/step_${y3}_renoise_xt.png";   if [[ -f "$prior" && -f "$data" && -f "$renoise" ]]; then     printf "file '%s'\n" "/home/vogechri/PixelGen/$prior" >> "$LIST";     printf "file '%s'\n" "/home/vogechri/PixelGen/$data" >> "$LIST";     printf "file '%s'\n" "/home/vogechri/PixelGen/$renoise" >> "$LIST";   else     echo "Missing at step $y3" >&2;     exit 1;   fi; done
 3412  ffmpeg -v error -y -f concat -safe 0 -i "$LIST" -r 10 -pix_fmt yuv420p "$OUT"
 3413  stat -c 'OUT_BYTES=%s' "$OUT"
 3414  wc -l "$LIST"
 3415  SRC="outpaint_images/test_intermediates"
 3416  SEQ_DIR="$SRC/ordered_frames_001_099"
 3417  OUT="$SRC/steps_triplet_001_099_exact.mp4"
 3418  rm -rf "$SEQ_DIR"
 3419  mkdir -p "$SEQ_DIR"
 3420  idx=1
 3421  for y in $(seq 1 99); do   y3=$(printf "%03d" "$y");   for kind in prior_x0_hat data_x0_proj renoise_xt; do     src="$SRC/step_${y3}_${kind}.png";     if [[ ! -f "$src" ]]; then       echo "Missing file: $src" >&2;       exit 1;     fi;     dst="$SEQ_DIR/frame_$(printf "%04d" "$idx").png";     ln -s "$(realpath "$src")" "$dst";     idx=$((idx+1));   done; done
 3422  # Encode with explicit sequence input.
 3423  ffmpeg -v error -y -framerate 6 -i "$SEQ_DIR/frame_%04d.png" -c:v libx264 -pix_fmt yuv420p "$OUT"
 3424  y first 9 frame mappings and counts.

 3426  echo "FRAME_COUNT=$(find "$SEQ_DIR" -maxdepth 1 -name 'frame_*.png' | wc -l)"
 3427  ls -lh "$OUT"
 3428  cd /home/vogechri/PixelGen
 3429  python - <<'PY'
 3430  from pathlib import Path
 3431  import os
 3432  base = Path('outpaint_images/test_intermediates')
 3433  out_seq = base / '_ordered_3x25_frames'
 3434  out_seq.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 3435  # clean previous frame links/files
 3436  for p in out_seq.glob('frame_*.png'):
 3437      p.unlink()
 3438  suffixes = ['prior_x0_hat', 'data_x0_proj', 'renoise_xt']
 3439  missing = []
 3440  idx = 1
 3441  for step in range(1, 26):
 3442      for suf in suffixes:
 3443          src = base / f'step_{step:03d}_{suf}.png'
 3444          if not src.exists():
 3445              missing.append(str(src))
 3446              continue
 3447          dst = out_seq / f'frame_{idx:05d}.png'
 3448          os.symlink(os.path.relpath(src, out_seq), dst)
 3449          idx += 1
 3450  print(f'Expected frames: {25*3}')
 3451  print(f'Created frames : {idx-1}')
 3452  if missing:
 3453      print('Missing files:')
 3454      for m in missing:
 3455          print(m)
 3456      raise SystemExit(2)
 3457  print('Frame sequence ready:', out_seq)

 5966  python -c "
 5967  import math
 5968  def _plan_superres_hr_axis(output_length, tile_size=512, min_overlap=64):
 5969      if output_length <= tile_size:
 5970          return [(0, output_length)]
 5971      n_tiles = math.ceil(output_length / tile_size)
 5972      raw_overlap = (n_tiles * tile_size - output_length) / (n_tiles - 1)
 5973      overlap_hr = int(math.ceil(raw_overlap / 16.0)) * 16
 5974      if n_tiles > 1 and overlap_hr < min_overlap:
 5975          n_tiles += 1
 5976          raw_overlap = (n_tiles * tile_size - output_length) / (n_tiles - 1)
 5977          overlap_hr = int(math.ceil(raw_overlap / 16.0)) * 16
 5978      while (n_tiles - 1) * (tile_size - overlap_hr) + tile_size < output_length:
 5979          n_tiles += 1
 5980          raw_overlap = (n_tiles * tile_size - output_length) / (n_tiles - 1)
 5981          overlap_hr = int(math.ceil(raw_overlap / 16.0)) * 16
 5982      stride = tile_size - overlap_hr
 5983      spans = []
 5984      for i in range(n_tiles):
 5985          start = i * stride
 5986          end = start + tile_size
 5987          if end > output_length:
 5988              end = output_length
 5989              start = end - tile_size
 5990          spans.append((start, end))
 5991      return spans
 5992  for size in [512, 768, 1024, 1536, 2048]:
 5993      spans = _plan_superres_hr_axis(size)
 5994      overlaps = [spans[i][1] - spans[i+1][0] for i in range(len(spans)-1)] if len(spans)>1 else []
 5995      print(f'{size:5d}: {len(spans)} tiles, overlap={overlaps}, spans={spans}')
 5996  "
